# Tidemark cards (rules 1.0)

Generated by `tools/tidemark/v1/export.js` from the checklist (`manifest.json`) and the designs (`tools/tidemark/v1/design_*.js`, `balance.js`). Do not edit. Card text is generated from the same data the simulator plays.

Status: numbers and abilities **designed** 2026-10-19 and balanced by simulation; not canon until the owner approves and people have played. Rules: `RULES.md`.

Cost line: Influence cost, then the Allegiance requirement (for example *Land 2* needs two Land permanents in play; Dual cards need that many of either side).
